Abstract: Data-driven methods, including graph neural networks, have been studied for accelerating power flow calculations in recent years, but very little attention has been paid to the solution feasibility, which can be obtained by traditional solvers. This paper presents a Variational Graph Autoencoder (VGAE) that detects the power flow solution feasibility, using the IEEE 118-bus case, to assess the validity of the solutions provided by AI-driven solvers.

</details>


### [174] [Present but Rescaled: Chat-to-Agent Transfer of Additive Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09156)
*Lucas Pinto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型在从单轮对话迁移到ReAct智能体场景时，加法激活引导（additive steering）的行为效应会发生缩放，且缩放方向因模型而异，存在安全风险。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。研究对象是大语言模型（LLM）的激活引导（activation steering）在对话与智能体场景间的迁移行为，属于AI安全/可解释性方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与课题方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型激活引导（additive steering）的chat-to-agent迁移研究。场景：单轮对话 vs ReAct工具使用智能体。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 研究对象为Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B、Yi-1.5-9B等大语言模型。不涉及遥感目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用自建实验协议（uniform-protocol distribution），N=300样本。指标：效应量T值（amplification ratio）、置信区间CI。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 主要发现：加法激活引导从chat迁移到agent时行为耦合发生缩放，Qwen2.5-7B上拒绝绕过向量在agent中放大（T=1.45），Gemma-2-9B放大至2.00倍，Yi-1.5-9B衰减至0.43倍。消融实验表明方向性消融（directional ablation）不放大（T=0.93），而加法注入放大（T=1.50），差异20.1个点。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型大小、推理效率、边缘部署等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM安全对齐领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Additive activation steering (injecting a scaled residual-stream direction during generation) is calibrated almost entirely in single-turn chat, yet the models it targets are increasingly deployed as tool-using ReAct agents. We present the first systematic chat-to-agent transfer study of additive steering, coupling behavioral measurement with a representation read-out in a matched-information design: the same items rendered as plain chat or as a ReAct tool-use episode, with matched-norm random-direction controls and the transcript re-encoded every turn to exclude KV-cache contamination. Transfer is real but rescaled, and the right description is a dissociation: the injected direction reaches the late layers at near-full strength in every setting and model tested (install-site agent-over-chat ratios 0.83-1.16 across three families), while the behavioral coupling is reset per model and context. On Qwen2.5-7B a refusal bypass vector amplifies in the agent (T = 1.45, CI [1.20, 1.78], N = 300); across a powered uniform-protocol distribution the coupling spans amplification (Gemma-2-9B T = 2.00) to attenuation (Yi-1.5-9B T = 0.43, CI [0.29, 0.60]), with no universal constant and a single clean attenuator against a universal sign. Directional ablation of the same axis does not amplify (T = 0.93, CI including 1) while additive injection amplifies (T = 1.50), a 20.1-point gain difference (CI [13.4, 26.8]) that identifies an additive-specific mechanism. Two pre-registered instruments converge to localize the rescaling to the ReAct format scaffold, before any tool observation, rather than to the observation boundary where a dilution account would predict it. The safety implication is immediate and unpredictable: agentic deployment amplifies steering-based refusal bypass by up to 2.00x on some models while others attenuate, so a deployment cannot assume a given model is safe under additive steering.

</details>


### [175] [A Personalized Computational Framework for Assessing the Sufficiency of Partially Observed Data in Healthcare AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09165)
*Qingchu Jin,Felistas Mazhude,Jamie B. Rabb,Robert S. Kramer,Douglas B. Sawyer,Raimond L. Winslow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Feature Sufficiency Analysis (FSA)方法，用于判断不完整的临床特征子集是否足以达到AI模型的完整特征预测性能，面向医疗AI部署场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医疗AI中的临床特征充分性分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可勉强关联的是"部署"一词，但指的是医疗AI系统的临床部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：临床特征充分性分析（Feature Sufficiency Analysis）；场景：术后通气需求预测、10年死亡率预测；传感器/数据类型：患者临床特征数据；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及ML algorithms trained on patient data。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。其核心是特征子集充分性分析，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文讨论的是医疗AI系统的临床部署可行性，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 两个案例研究：1) 心脏手术患者术后通气需求预测数据集；2) 门诊患者10年死亡率预测数据集。评价指标：FFC (full-feature-capacity) 预测性能。

Experiments: 在两个医疗数据集上验证FSA方法，展示了特征排序、难预测患者识别、成本感知优化等能力。未与目标检测或遥感方法进行对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、加速或部署技术。论文本身局限包括：仅针对临床特征缺失场景，方法泛化性未在遥感/视觉任务上验证。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医疗AI领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Achieving early and timely diagnosis and treatment for disease is a major challenge. Recent applications of machine learning (ML) algorithms trained on patient data have shown promise in many different settings for predicting the patient health state. A challenge often faced when applying these ML algorithms is that at any given time, not all clinical variables (features) needed as input to perform prediction tasks are available. We define the concept of full-feature-capacity (FFC) to refer to prediction performance when such algorithms make use of all features on which they were trained. We then introduce Feature Sufficiency Analysis (FSA) - an analysis for determining whether a subset of all clinical features needed by an AI model is sufficient to achieve FFC. FSA estimates the underlying distributions of missing variables conditioned on features that are available. FSA provides a patient-specific assessment of whether the existing set of measured features achieves FFC. If yes, then there is no need to acquire further inputs and a ML-based prediction. We provide two case studies: prediction of need for postoperative prolonged ventilation in patients recovering from heart surgery; 10-year mortality prediction in an outpatient cohort. We also demonstrate that FSA also provides a clinically interpretable feature-ranking methodology based on prediction sufficiency, identifies intrinsically hard-to-predict patient populations, and has the potential to perform cost-aware optimization for clinical data acquisition. FSA provides a generic computational approach for determining whether incomplete clinical information is sufficient to support trustworthy AI-assisted clinical decision-making, thereby facilitating the prospective deployment of healthcare AI systems across diverse clinical settings.

</details>


### [176] [COAST: Context-Aware Differential Learning for Gene Expression Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09166)
*Keunho Byeon,Sunhong Park,Jeewoo Lim,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COAST框架，利用上下文感知的差分学习从H&E病理图像预测空间基因表达，核心创新在于同时建模绝对表达和相对表达差异。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于计算病理学/空间转录组学领域，涉及H&E组织病理图像与基因表达预测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：空间基因表达预测（spatial gene expression prediction）；场景：计算病理学（computational pathology）；传感器：H&E组织病理图像（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：基因表达值（非检测目标）。

Model architecture: COAST框架：包含局部和全局上下文特征提取、类型特定调制（type-specific modulation）、Transformer编码器聚合目标与上下文斑点的token。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个空间转录组学数据集（multiple spatial transcriptomics datasets）；指标：基于相关性（correlation-based）和基于分布（distribution-based）的指标。

Experiments: 在多个空间转录组学数据集上，COAST在相关性和分布指标上取得一致改进。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物信息学/计算病理学方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics enables profiling of spatial gene expression but is limited by high cost and low throughput, motivating prediction from H&E histopathology images. Existing context-aware methods mainly supervise absolute expression, while relative expression relationships between spots are rarely used explicitly. We propose COAST, a context-aware differential learning framework for spatial gene expression prediction. COAST conditions the local and global context features with type-specific modulation and aggregates the target and context spot tokens using a Transformer encoder to capture both fine-grained local patterns and slide-level structure. It is trained with a joint objective that combines absolute expression regression with signed differential regression between the target and context spots. Experiments on multiple spatial transcriptomics datasets show consistent improvements in correlation- and distribution-based metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of context-aware differential learning for histology-based spatial gene expression prediction.

</details>


### [177] [Understanding Schedule-Free Methods in Nonconvex Optimization: Rate Guarantees and Escaping Saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09167)
*Jiseok Chae,Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了Schedule-Free梯度下降法在非凸优化中的最优最坏情况收敛率，并证明了其严格鞍点回避性质。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于优化算法（Schedule-Free梯度下降）的收敛理论分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体任务、应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型结构或网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论分析，未提及实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 论文为纯理论收敛性分析，未讨论实际应用场景、计算效率或部署可行性。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Schedule-Free方法本身作为一种免调度器的优化器，理论上可用于训练轻量化遥感目标检测模型以减少超参数调优成本，但论文仅提供理论收敛证明，未涉及任何检测任务或轻量化场景，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Schedule-Free methods have attracted growing interest for alleviating the burden of designing and tuning a learning rate scheduler, while matching and sometimes even outperforming optimizers with tuned schedulers. Despite their strong empirical results, their convergence theory in nonconvex optimization, where modern machine learning objectives typically arise, has remained largely unexplored. In this paper, we provide worst-case analyses of Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent, in their standard form and without auxiliary modifications or restrictive conditions, for smooth but possibly nonconvex objectives. Based on a Lyapunov analysis derived from the continuous-time limiting ordinary differential equation associated with these methods, we show that Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent achieve the optimal worst-case convergence rates attainable among first-order methods. We further formulate Schedule-Free gradient descent as a nonautonomous dynamical system and prove strict-saddle avoidance under an arbitrarily small one-time perturbation. These theoretical results provide a better understanding of the strong performance that Schedule-Free methods demonstrate.

</details>


### [178] [Application of machine learning to monster level prediction in tabletop RPG game design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09196)
*Jolanta Śliwa,Jakub Adamczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究桌游角色扮演游戏（TTRPG）中怪物属性与等级之间的序数回归预测问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是桌游（TTRPG）怪物平衡性设计中的序数回归预测问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化等方向完全无关。既不是遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及任何模型压缩或边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：序数回归（ordinal regression），预测怪物等级；场景：桌游角色扮演游戏（TTRPG）设计；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 比较了经典回归模型、专用序数回归算法、树集成模型（tree-based ensembles）、神经网络（neural networks with ordinal-aware losses）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建的Pathfinder Second Edition怪物数据集（首次用于TTRPG怪物等级预测）；评价指标：序数排序指标（ordinal ranking）和预测准确率（predictive accuracy）。

Experiments: 树集成模型（如随机森林/梯度提升树）优于线性模型和神经网络，在序数排序和预测准确率上接近完美；可解释AI分析表明模型与人类直觉和游戏规则一致。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无。该论文的研究对象（桌游怪物属性预测）、任务（序数回归）、场景（游戏设计）与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何交集，不具备可借鉴的技术点。

Abstract: Designing balanced adversaries is a central but labor-intensive task in tabletop role-playing game (TTRPG) development. In systems such as Pathfinder, each monster is described by many numerical attributes that jointly determine its power, summarized as an ordinal level. We investigate whether machine learning can support designers by predicting this level from a monster's attributes, framing the task as tabular ordinal regression. We introduce what is, to our knowledge, the first dataset built specifically for TTRPG monster-level prediction, derived from publicly available Pathfinder Second Edition data. Using it, we compare classical regression models with rounding schemes, dedicated tabular ordinal regression algorithms, and neural networks with ordinal-aware losses. To mirror real design workflows, we evaluate all models under chronological and expanding-window protocols with several complementary metrics. Results show that tree-based ensembles outperform linear models and neural approaches, achieving near-perfect ordinal ranking and high predictive accuracy. Explainable AI analyses, such as feature importance and error distributions, show that the model is aligned with human intuition and follows patterns grounded in game rules. Together, these results show that machine learning can reliably approximate designer judgments and serve as an effective computer-aided tool for monster balancing and broader TTRPG system design.

</details>


### [179] [Interference and Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09202)
*Julius Störk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于任务间干扰建模的持续学习方法IGFA，通过任务感知正交化实现无回放、无Fisher矩阵的遗忘控制。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是持续学习(continual learning)中的灾难性遗忘问题，属于通用机器学习/深度学习领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也未讨论任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：持续学习(continual learning)中的遗忘建模。应用场景：通用图像分类/任务流。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于frozen-feature regime下的干扰能量建模，提出Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA)方法。核心思想是通过任务感知正交化(task-aware orthogonalization)在任务对齐时共享方向、冲突时保护方向。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及"across benchmarks"和与"strongest replay-free structural baselines"对比。

Experiments: 在 dissimilar-task streams 上匹配了最强的无回放结构基线方法，在相似任务上优于无条件投影方法。实现了结构可分离任务的无损保留，以及不可避免代价从不可逆遗忘向可恢复可塑性的转移。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 未讨论实时推理或边缘部署场景；4. 方法基于frozen-feature假设，在需要特征微调的场景下适用性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。持续学习中的干扰建模思想（任务间方向对齐/冲突）在理论上可迁移到遥感在轨场景中模型持续适应新数据分布的问题，但本文未提供任何与遥感、目标检测或轻量化直接相关的可借鉴点。

Abstract: Continual learning commonly relies on post-hoc mechanisms such as replay, elastic regularization, or distillation. This work argues that forgetting should instead be modeled directly as interference between tasks. In the frozen-feature regime, forgetting from learning a new task is exactly the interference energy induced on the old task. In deep networks, the same quantity is recovered through path-averaged curvature with minimal additional forward passes.
  When task supports are disjoint, forgetting can be eliminated structurally and when task supports overlap in conflicting directions, a non-zero distortion floor is unavoidable. The same geometry optimally merges models through task-aware orthogonalization. From this analysis we derive Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA), a replay-free, Fisher-free method that shares directions when tasks align and protects them when they conflict. Across benchmarks, IGFA achieves lossless retention when tasks are structurally separable and moves unavoidable cost from irreversible forgetting into deferred but recoverable plasticity when they are not. It matches the strongest replay-free structural baselines on dissimilar-task streams and improves on unconditional projection when similarity makes transfer worth preserving.

</details>


### [180] [Temporal Knowledge Graph Forecasting under Distribution Shifts: A Synthetic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09232)
*Konrad Özdemir,Julia Gastinger,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究时序知识图谱（TKG）预测模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，使用合成数据生成器控制递归、同质性和周期性三种机制，评估七种预测架构在平稳和偏移场景下的表现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是时序知识图谱（TKG）预测，属于知识图谱与图神经网络领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。没有任何遥感图像、目标检测、在轨部署或轻量化方法的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为时序知识图谱预测（TKG forecasting），场景为合成数据生成的分布偏移环境，不涉及遥感传感器、目标类别或场景理解。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构细节，仅提及评估了七种预测架构（seven forecasting architectures）以及基于记忆的基线模型（memory-based baselines）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等内容）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成TKG数据生成器（synthetic TKG generator），编码递归（recurrence）、同质性（homophily）和周期性（periodicity）三种机制。未说明具体评价指标。

Experiments: 在平稳（stationary）和偏移（shifting）两种机制下评估七种预测架构。实验发现：递归和周期性规律在平稳条件下可恢复；基于记忆的基线在递归主导数据时具有竞争力；结构断裂（structural breaks）对模型适应性构成挑战，潜在实体-社区结构偏移是最难应对的。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。该论文的分布偏移鲁棒性分析思路可能对泛化性研究有启发，但与本研究课题无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是时序知识图谱预测的鲁棒性，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化在问题域、方法、评价指标上均无交集。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) represent evolving relational systems, whose underlying data-generating processes often change over time. Yet, TKG forecasting models are commonly evaluated only on empirical benchmark datasets that provide limited insight into the models' robustness to such distribution shifts. Recognising this issue, we study TKG forecasting under controlled shift environments using a synthetic TKG generator that encodes three temporal and structural properties -- recurrence, homophily, and periodicity -- as data-generating mechanisms. This allows us to evaluate seven forecasting architectures under stationary and shifting regimes. Our experiments suggest that robustness in TKG forecasting is highly signal-dependent. Recurrence-based and periodic regularities are largely recoverable under stationary conditions, and simple memory-based baselines can be competitive when recurrence dominates the data. However, structural breaks reveal limitations in model adaptivity, with shifts in latent entity-community structure posing the strongest challenge in our study. Overall, our findings improve the understanding of the capabilities and limitations of current TKG models confronted with temporal distribution shifts.

</details>


### [181] [All you need is SAMPAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09235)
*Jayadeva,Madhur Aswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为SAMPAT的三层可解释神经网络架构，通过多元多项式和解析变换实现任意光滑函数的逼近与完全可解释性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于神经网络的可解释性（interpretability），提出一种基于多项式近似的可解释神经网络架构SAMPAT，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别，仅提及合成数据和基准数据集上的实验。

Model architecture: SAMPAT（Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations）：三层神经网络架构，可学习连续、处处可微的函数，输出为封闭紧凑的代数/解析表达式。支持限制神经元连接以生成特定形式（正则多项式、三角多项式、有理表达式、高斯混合等），也可无限制地自主学习结构。加入跳跃连接后，4-6层可表示AI/ML中广泛使用的方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在合成数据和基准数据集上实验，性能具有竞争力。

Experiments: 在合成数据和基准数据集上，SAMPAT以更简单的表示取得了有竞争力的性能。对于许多任务，两层SAMPAT即可满足需求。

Limitations: 论文未讨论在遥感或目标检测任务上的适用性；未提供计算效率、参数量等实际部署指标；可解释性与检测精度/速度的权衡未分析。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。SAMPAT的可解释性思路（多项式解析表达）理论上可用于理解遥感目标检测模型的决策过程，但论文未涉及检测任务或轻量化，且多项式近似的计算开销在星上资源受限场景下可能不具优势。

Abstract: The current state of the art in AI/ML rests on deep neural architectures, which, in general, suffer from a lack of interpretability. Interpretability is crucial to gleaning insights while analyzing experimental data, where quantitative predictions may not be adequate for a scientist. We present a three layer neural architecture, SAMPAT (Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations), that can provably learn a continuous, everywhere differentiable function, that can approximate any smooth function arbitrarily closely. SAMPAT's approximant can be expressed as a closed and compact algebraic, analytic expression, providing complete interpretability. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets indicate that SAMPAT yields competitive performance with simpler representations. For many tasks, a two layer SAMPAT suffices. By imposing restrictions on the connectivity between neurons, SAMPAT may be used to provide a range of approximants, including regular and trigonometric polynomials, rational expressions, Gaussians, mixtures of Gaussians, as well as arbitrary combinations of the same; without restrictions, it learns a suitable structure. SAMPAT may be used to factorize polynomials and model nonlinear systems. With the addition of skip connections, a 4 to 6 layer SAMPAT is adequate to represent a substantive range of methods widely used in AI/ML, allowing the choice of a model's family, not just its parameters, to also be optimized as part of the learning process.

</details>


### [182] [Forget Narrowly, Retain Broadly: Unlearning as an Asymmetric Generalization Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09236)
*Amit Peleg,Naman Deep Singh,Naama Pearl,Bibhabasu Mohapatra,Matthias Hein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUITE评估协议和JensUn++算法，解决大语言模型机器遗忘中的不对称泛化问题（欠遗忘和过遗忘），与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM）的机器遗忘（machine unlearning）问题，属于自然语言处理/隐私安全领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM机器遗忘（知识移除）；场景：隐私保护与安全；完全不涉及遥感、目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，提及在三个LLM上验证JensUn++算法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 提出SUITE评估协议和训练语料库，用于评估LLM遗忘效果。指标关注forget-retain utility trade-off。

Experiments: 在三个LLM上验证JensUn++算法，在顺序遗忘和联合遗忘场景下均取得最佳forget-retain trade-off。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM隐私安全领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in LLMs is the targeted removal of specific knowledge while preserving all other capabilities, critical for privacy and safety. Yet existing benchmarks measure it unreliably. They miss knowledge that resurfaces under paraphrased or indirect queries, a failure we call under-forgetting, and lack the semantic, syntactic, and lexical probes needed to verify that unrelated knowledge is preserved, a failure we call over-forgetting. Both failures reflect an asymmetric generalization problem. Forget evaluation must cover diverse query formulations of the same target facts, testing whether forgetting holds beyond exact training prompts. Retain evaluation must probe a far larger and implicitly defined set, namely every fact disjoint from the forget target. The retain set thus defines the effective forget set, yet current datasets provide no fine-grained annotation of this forget-retain boundary. We address this with SUITE, an evaluation protocol and training corpus that captures forget-retain structure for real-world factual domains. Methods trained on SUITE improve substantially, showing that training data is as important as algorithmic design. Building on the obtained insights, we introduce JensUn++, an unlearning algorithm that achieves the best forget-retain utility trade-off across three LLMs, in both sequential and joint unlearning settings. Code and datasets are available at https://amitpeleg.github.io/forget-narrowly-retain-broadly

</details>


### [183] [LionVote: Per-Layer Learning Rate Adaptation for Lion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09266)
*Kris Atallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LionVote，一种逐层学习率调节机制，通过梯度方向稳定性和动量健康度两个诊断指标为每层参数调整学习率，在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上超越Lion和AdamW。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于优化器层面的逐层学习率调节机制（LionVote），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务或任何模型轻量化/部署技术。虽然ViT架构在遥感领域有应用，但本文仅以ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100作为验证平台，属于通用视觉优化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（CIFAR-100）；应用场景：通用视觉；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：CIFAR-100的100类通用物体。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: ViT-Tiny（Vision Transformer小型变体），以及均匀CNN架构。不涉及遥感专用检测模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文是优化器方法（逐层学习率调节），不是模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。LionVote作为优化器机制，在训练阶段使用，不直接影响推理部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-100（通用视觉数据集，非遥感）；评价指标：Top-1准确率；统计检验：Welch's t-test（p < 0.02）。

Experiments: ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上：LionVote 69.7% vs. Lion 69.0% vs. AdamW 68.8% Top-1准确率。在均匀CNN架构上，经SGD+余弦退火调优后LionVote优势不明显；ViT架构上收益与任务相关。

Limitations: 1. 仅在CIFAR-100小规模数据集上验证，未在遥感大数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR）上测试；2. 仅验证了ViT-Tiny，未在遥感常用检测模型（如YOLO系列、RetinaNet、Faster R-CNN）上测试；3. 未涉及遥感目标检测的旋转框、小目标等核心挑战；4. 未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 逐层学习率调节思想可迁移到遥感检测模型的微调阶段，特别是轻量化模型（如MobileNet、ShuffleNet变体）不同层对学习率敏感度不同；2. 梯度方向稳定性和动量健康度两个诊断指标可尝试用于遥感小目标检测训练中的学习率调度优化。但整体而言，该工作与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的核心问题（检测精度-速度权衡、星上资源约束、模型压缩）无直接关联。

Abstract: Per-layer diagnostics reveal that, at the prescribed learning rate, Lion's effective scale is 2.6-2.8x too high for attention and MLP parameters and ~2x too high for normalization layers on ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100; this 32% cross-layer-type disparity cannot be reproduced by a single global rate. The measurement comes from LionVote, a per-layer learning rate mechanism in which each parameter tensor maintains a compound level, a persistent integer updated every c epochs by two diagnostics (gradient direction stability and momentum health) resolved by a validation loss tiebreaker. Voting thresholds derive from geometric identities, the EMA time constant, and a noise-floor estimate; cadence is bounded structurally and selected by ablation. On ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100, LionVote achieves 69.7% top-1 accuracy vs. Lion's 69.0% (p < 0.02, Welch's t-test) and AdamW's 68.8%. Per-layer adaptation value depends on both architectural heterogeneity and task; on uniform CNN architectures tuned SGD with cosine annealing remains dominant, and on ViT architectures gains are task-dependent.

</details>


### [184] [Autoregressive latent diffusion for 3D molecule generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09277)
*Federico Ottomano,Gaopeng Ren,Yingzhen Li,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KRONOS是一个潜在自回归扩散框架，用于在预训练自编码器的潜在空间中生成分子，联合建模分子图拓扑和几何结构，并支持无条件与片段条件生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是3D分子生成（molecule generation），属于计算化学/药物发现领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等方向没有任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D分子生成；应用场景：药物发现/计算化学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：分子图拓扑与几何结构。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: KRONOS框架：潜在自回归扩散模型（latent autoregressive diffusion），基于预训练自编码器的潜在空间，联合建模分子图拓扑和几何结构。采用Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) 训练策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：QM9、GEOM-Drugs；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称（如分子生成领域的有效性、唯一性、新颖性等指标未列出）。

Experiments: 在QM9和GEOM-Drugs上，KRONOS在自回归方法中达到领先的无条件生成性能，与扩散模型竞争力相当；片段条件生成对无条件生成性能影响可忽略。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，没有任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。属于跨领域（计算化学）方法论文。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于分子生成领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) molecule generation has been dominated by diffusion models, which achieve strong generation quality but typically require the molecular size to be specified a priori. Recent autoregressive approaches have substantially narrowed the performance gap while naturally supporting variable-length generation and conditioning on partial molecular context. However, balancing unconditional and context-conditioned generation remains challenging. We introduce KRONOS, a latent autoregressive diffusion framework that generates molecules in the latent space of a pre-trained autoencoder, jointly modeling molecular graph topology and geometry, while retaining the flexibility of autoregressive generation. We further introduce a mixed training strategy inspired by Fill-in-the Middle (FIM) paradigm, enabling both unconditional and fragment-conditioned molecular generation within a single left-to-right autoregressive model. Experiments on QM9 and GEOM-Drugs demonstrate that KRONOS achieves leading unconditional generation performance among autoregressive methods, while remaining competitive with diffusion models. Moreover, fragment-conditioned generation is achieved with negligible impact on unconditional generation performance, demonstrating that both generation paradigms can be supported within a single architecture.

</details>


### [185] [Super-Tuning: From Activation-Aware Pruning to Sparse Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09287)
*Ivan Ilin,Philip Zmushko,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用剪枝显著性信号（Wanda评分）为PEFT选择稀疏可训练子集（Super），并与LoRA结合（Supra），在LLM微调中降低计算开销。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的参数高效微调（PEFT），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型参数高效微调（PEFT）；应用场景：LLM微调（数学推理）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Super：基于Wanda-style激活加权幅度评分选择稀疏可训练支撑集；Supra：将稀疏更新与LoRA结合的混合适配器，通过预算拆分规则保持可训练参数预算一致。

Lightweight method: 方法本质是参数高效微调（PEFT），借鉴了剪枝中的显著性信号（Wanda评分、幅度评分）来选择稀疏可训练子集，但并非模型压缩/轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等），而是微调策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"减少内存、计算和每任务存储"，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Math17K（算术推理）；评价指标：平均准确率（average accuracy）；模型：Llama-3.2-1B、Meta-Llama-3-8B。

Experiments: 在Math17K算术实验上，Super/Supra变体在单种子设置下达到测试配置中的最高平均准确率；与PaFi-style纯幅度基线对比，发现低评分支撑集在幅度和Wanda排序下均有效。

Limitations: 1. 仅在LLM微调场景验证，未涉及遥感图像或目标检测任务；2. 无部署相关指标（速度、参数量、FLOPs等）；3. 方法依赖校准集计算显著性评分，引入额外开销；4. 未讨论在视觉模型或遥感场景下的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：1. "将剪枝显著性信号复用于参数选择"的思路，理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的稀疏微调或增量学习场景；2. Wanda-style评分（激活×权重幅度）可作为一种轻量化的参数重要性评估方式；3. 稀疏更新+低秩适配器的混合策略（Supra）对星上模型增量更新可能有参考价值。但整体方向差异大，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain expensive to fine-tune because full-parameter updates require substantial memory, compute, and per-task storage. We study whether saliency signals originally developed for pruning can be reused to choose where a model should adapt. We propose Super, a sparse parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method that fixes a small trainable support using a Wanda-style activation-weighted magnitude score [Sun et al., 2023] computed from a calibration pass. We then introduce Supra, a hybrid adapter that combines this sparse update with LoRA while preserving a matched trainable-parameter budget through a simple budget-splitting rule. In single-seed Math17K arithmetic experiments on Llama-3.2-1B and Meta-Llama-3-8B, the best Super/Supra variants achieve the highest average accuracy among the tested schedule-selected adapter configurations. We also include a PaFi-style magnitude-only support as a closest training-free sparse baseline and find that low-score supports under both magnitude and Wanda-style orderings can be effective. These results suggest that simple pruning-inspired orderings can provide useful fixed sparse supports for PEFT, especially when combined with low-rank adapters.

</details>


### [186] [Risk-Aware General-Utility Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09298)
*Pedro P. Santos,Fábio Vital,Alberto Sardinha,Francisco S. Melo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通用马尔可夫决策过程（GUMDP）中的风险感知目标优化，提出基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的在线规划方法求解熵风险度量（ERM）目标。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是强化学习中的风险感知决策优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星场景。任务类型为风险感知的马尔可夫决策过程（GUMDP）优化，涉及标准MDP、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习、多目标MDP等，与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的在线规划方法，用于求解风险感知GUMDP。不涉及任何视觉模型或检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。实验涉及多种任务（标准MDP、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习、多目标MDP），但未提及遥感相关数据集或目标检测评价指标（如mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 实验展示了该方法在优化风险感知行为方面的成功，但未与任何遥感目标检测方法进行对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未讨论模型在资源受限设备上的部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。如果强行关联，MCTS作为一种在线规划方法，理论上可用于遥感卫星的自主任务规划或决策，但与目标检测模型轻量化这一核心课题无关。

Abstract: We study general-utility Markov decision processes (GUMDPs) with risk-aware objectives. In this framework, an agent aims to optimize a risk measure of the distribution of objective values, where the objective function depends on the frequency of visitation of states induced by the agent's policy. First, we motivate, propose, and formalize risk-aware GUMDPs, which enable agents and decision makers to trade off expected performance by risk aversion while benefiting from the rich set of objectives that can be cast under the framework of GUMDPs. We focus our attention on the entropic risk measure (ERM). Second, we show how we can solve risk-aware GUMDPs with ERM objectives by resorting to online planning techniques. In particular, we propose an approach based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to provably solve risk-aware GUMDPs up to any desired accuracy. Third, we provide a set of experimental results showcasing that our approach is successful when optimizing for a spectrum of risk-aware behaviors in the context of GUMDPs under diverse tasks (standard MDPs, maximum state entropy exploration, imitation learning, and multi-objective MDPs).

</details>


### [187] [Graph Neural Networks for Scalable and Transferable Node Centrality Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09372)
*Samra Sana,Giorgio Mantica,Saul Imbrici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究用GNN近似计算图中心性（介数中心性和接近中心性），将其建模为节点排序问题，关注跨图拓扑的可迁移性，并在合成图和真实图上验证了推理加速效果。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是图神经网络（GNN）用于图中心性近似计算，属于图机器学习/图数据挖掘领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然提到了推理加速（97.7x speedup），但这是相对于精确中心性计算而言，并非针对深度学习模型本身的轻量化压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图中心性近似计算（节点排序）；场景：图数据分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务或场景。

Model architecture: 消息传递型GNN（message-passing GNNs）。摘要未说明具体GNN变体（如GCN、GAT等）的细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到GNN推理相比精确计算有97.7x加速，但这属于算法替代带来的计算加速，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到推理速度提升（97.7x speedup over exact computation），但这是相对于精确算法的加速比，不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Erdos-Renyi图、Barabasi-Albert图、Gaussian Random Partition图、真实世界图拓扑；评价指标：Kendall's tau秩相关系数（评估排序质量）。摘要未说明训练/测试集规模细节。

Experiments: 在未见过的Erdos-Renyi图上，介数中心性tau=0.851，接近中心性tau=0.894；大规模介数模型（N=5000节点）tau=0.938；混合分布训练提升了跨图家族的迁移性；接近中心性对社区结构图敏感，迁移到真实拓扑时表现下降。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 接近中心性在真实拓扑上的迁移性不足，作者也将其列为开放挑战；3. 未涉及任何轻量化压缩方法；4. 仅在合成图和少量真实图上验证，未在遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：（1）GNN在遥感场景中可用于场景图理解或多目标关系建模，但本文不涉及；（2）混合分布训练提升跨域迁移性的思路，可类比遥感多源数据（不同传感器、不同分辨率）的域适应训练；（3）推理加速的思路（用学习替代精确计算）在遥感中可类比用轻量网络替代复杂检测头。但总体而言，本文与课题方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) provide a learning-based framework for approximating graph quantities that are expensive to compute exactly. This paper investigates GNNs for scalable approximation of betweenness and closeness centrality, formulated as a node-ranking problem. Exact centrality values are used as supervision, and ranking quality is evaluated using Kendall's tau rank correlation. We study whether message-passing GNNs can learn transferable structural representations across different graph topologies rather than only fitting the distribution used during training. On unseen Erdos renyi graphs, the proposed models achieve tau = 0.851 for betweenness and tau = 0.894 for closeness. A large-scale betweenness model trained on graphs with N = 5,000 nodes achieves tau = 0.938, demonstrating scalability. Mixed-distribution training on Erdos renyi, Barabasi-Albert, and Gaussian Random Partition graphs improves betweenness transfer across graph families. In contrast, closeness centrality remains more sensitive to community-structured graphs and shows reduced transfer to real-world topologies. Finally, GNN inference achieves up to a 97.7x speedup over exact computation. These results show that mixed-distribution training can improve structural transfer in GNN-based centrality approximation, while identifying closeness centrality's sensitivity to topology as an open challenge.

</details>


### [188] [Mach-Mind-4-Flash Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09375)
*Foundation Model Team*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个35B参数（3B激活参数）的MoE智能体模型Mach-Mind-4-Flash，通过后训练优化和多阶段流水线（统一RL训练框架、多教师策略蒸馏、混合长度策略优化）实现与100B参数模型相当的性能，并压缩推理链长度19-46%。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体（agent）能力优化和推理链压缩，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然涉及模型压缩（推理链压缩19-46%），但这是针对LLM推理链的token效率优化，而非视觉模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对MoE架构或LLM推理压缩有跨领域兴趣。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型智能体（agent）推理与训练优化；应用场景：通用AI智能体、数学推理（AIME）、指令跟随（IFBench）、安全对齐、函数调用（BFCL）、中文浏览（BrowseComp-zh）等；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构，总参数量35B，激活参数量3B。采用多教师策略蒸馏（MOPD）和混合长度策略优化（HMPO）。

Lightweight method: 推理链压缩：Hybrid Median-length Policy Optimization (HMPO) 单阶段token效率方法，压缩推理链长度19-46%，精度损失≤0.7个百分点。注意：这是LLM推理链压缩，不是视觉模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。模型规模35B参数（3B激活）对于星上部署而言仍然过大，不适用于遥感卫星在轨场景。未提供FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 评估基准：AIME'26 (92.70)、IFBench (82.82)、Behavioral-SafetyBench (80.74)、BFCL-v4 (75.80)、BrowseComp-zh (72.31)、ClawBench (84.20)。均为LLM智能体能力评估基准，与遥感目标检测无关。

Experiments: 与100B参数类模型对比，性能相当或超越。训练加速17%（端到端）。推理链压缩19-46%且精度损失≤0.7个百分点。未提供与遥感目标检测相关的实验。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测领域；2. 模型规模（35B参数）不适合星上部署；3. 轻量化方法（HMPO）针对LLM推理链，无法直接迁移到视觉目标检测模型；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) MoE架构的"总参数大但激活参数小"的设计哲学，可探索遥感目标检测的MoE轻量检测头设计；2) 多教师策略蒸馏（MOPD）中消除混合奖励seesaw退化的思路，可类比到多尺度/多类别遥感目标检测中的知识蒸馏策略设计；3) 推理链压缩的token效率思想，可类比到遥感检测模型中冗余特征图/通道的压缩。整体而言，直接可迁移性较低。

Abstract: We present Mach-Mind-4-Flash, a 35B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) agentic model with 3B activated parameters. Through post-training optimization alone without scaling pre-training compute, the model achieves performance on par with or surpassing that of 100B-parameter-class models. By introducing scalable agentic interaction environments for large-scale reinforcement learning, the model attains significant performance gains on real-world application tasks. Our pipeline comprises three stages: (1) a unified RL/OPD training infrastructure with dynamic multi-teacher scheduling and operator-level acceleration, delivering 17\% end-to-end training speedup; (2) multiple domain-specific RL experts trained in parallel across Reasoning, General, and Agent tracks, then fused into a single generalist via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) -- a routed reverse-KL objective that eliminates the see-saw degradation of mixed-reward RL; (3) Hybrid Median-length Policy Optimization (HMPO), a single-stage token-efficiency method that compresses reasoning chains by 19--46\% with $\le$0.7 percentage-point accuracy loss. Mach-Mind-4-Flash scores 92.70 on AIME'26, 82.82 on IFBench, 80.74 on Behavioral-SafetyBench, 75.80 on BFCL-v4, 72.31 on BrowseComp-zh, and 84.20 on ClawBench -- leading or matching models with 10--30$\times$ its activated size at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [189] [Learning Physics-Informed Surrogate Model of Linear Elastic Displacement Fields from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09382)
*Rodolphe Barlogis,Ferhat Tamssaouet,Quentin Falcoz,Stéphane Grieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息驱动的DeepONet框架，用于断裂弹性域的实时结构健康监测，不依赖有限元训练数据，通过局部惩罚项弱施加断裂边界无牵引条件。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于结构健康监测（structural health monitoring）和断裂力学（fracture mechanics），属于固体力学/计算力学领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：实时结构健康监测（real-time structural health monitoring）；场景：断裂弹性域（fractured elastic domains）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: Physics-informed DeepONet框架，包含针对断裂几何的专用编码策略（dedicated encoding strategy），通过局部惩罚项（localized penalty term）弱施加断裂边界无牵引条件（traction-free condition）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然论文提到"fast surrogate model"，但这里的"fast"是指替代有限元仿真的快速代理模型，而非面向边缘/星上部署的模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然目标是"real-time"监测，但未涉及星上或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提到"presented numerical example focuses on one representative fracture geometry"，使用数值算例而非遥感数据集。

Experiments: 仅展示了一个代表性断裂几何的数值算例，验证了公式的可行性。未与任何目标检测或遥感方法进行对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅验证了单一断裂几何，未推广到多样化的断裂几何；3. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；4. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：该工作使用物理信息神经网络（PINN）替代传统数值仿真，这种"代理模型"思路在遥感领域或许可用于替代复杂的辐射传输模型或大气校正模型，但与在轨目标检测模型轻量化这一核心课题无直接关系。

Abstract: This work aims to develop a fast and physically consistent surrogate model for real-time structural health monitoring of fractured elastic domains. We propose a physics-informed DeepONet framework that predicts displacement fields from both boundary conditions and fracture geometry, using a dedicated encoding strategy for the latter and without relying on finite-element-generated training data. The traction-free condition on the fracture boundary is imposed weakly through a localized penalty term. The presented numerical example focuses on one representative fracture geometry, demonstrating the feasibility of the formulation and laying the groundwork for extensions to surrogate modeling across diverse fracture geometries.

</details>


### [190] [Fully Trainable Deep Differentiable Logic Gate Networks and Lookup Table Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09399)
*Wout Mommen,Lars Keuninckx,Matthias Hartmann,Werner Van Leekwijck,Piet Wambacq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对可微逻辑门网络(LGN)和查找表网络(LUTN)中连接进行部分/完全优化的训练方法，在MNIST等小规模基准上以更少逻辑门和参数取得更高精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于逻辑门网络和查找表网络的连接优化训练方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未提及任何轻量化部署相关技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）。虽然逻辑门网络本身具有硬件友好的潜力，但论文未讨论遥感或目标检测应用。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（Yin-Yang, MNIST, Fashion-MNIST）；应用场景：通用小规模图像分类；传感器类型：无；目标类别：手写数字、时尚物品等，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 可微逻辑门网络(LGN)和查找表网络(LUTN)；每门/LUT输入引脚上使用连接概率分布，选择最优连接；同时学习最优门类型或LUT条目；使用高学习率、straight-through estimators和修剪常量输出门类型来保证训练稳定性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。但逻辑门网络本身是一种极低比特的神经网络形式，连接优化可减少逻辑门数量（50倍减少），具有潜在的硬件效率优势。然而论文未讨论在遥感或目标检测场景下的轻量化部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到逻辑门数量减少（50倍）和可训练参数减少（4倍），但未给出具体数值或与遥感在轨部署相关的分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Yin-Yang, MNIST Handwritten Digits (98.92%), Fashion-MNIST；评价指标：分类准确率(accuracy)。未涉及遥感数据集（如DOTA, DIOR, HRSC2016, SSDD等）或目标检测指标（mAP, FPS等）。

Experiments: 两层8000门LGN在MNIST上达98.92%；单层8000门达98.45%（比固定连接LGN少50倍门）；LUTN两层2000个6输入LUT达98.88%；训练稳定性验证到10层（LGN）和6层（LUTN）。

Limitations: 1. 仅在极小规模图像分类数据集上验证，未在遥感大图或目标检测任务上测试；2. 未讨论模型在遥感卫星场景下的适用性；3. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标；4. 逻辑门网络在大规模遥感图像上的可扩展性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 1. 逻辑门网络(LGN)的极低比特特性对星上FPGA部署有潜在启发，但需验证其在遥感目标检测任务上的可行性；2. 连接优化思想可类比于结构化剪枝，但需适配CNN/Transformer架构；3. 该方法目前仅适用于分类任务，扩展到旋转框检测等遥感特有任务需要大量改造；4. 整体借鉴价值有限，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method for both partial and full optimization of the connections in deep differentiable logic gate networks (LGNs) and lookup table networks (LUTNs). Our training method utilizes a probability distribution over a set of connections per gate/lookup table (LUT) input pin, selecting the connection with highest merit, all whilst the optimal gate types or LUT-entries are learned in parallel. We show that the connection-optimized LGNs outperform standard fixed-connection LGNs on the Yin-Yang, MNIST Handwritten Digits and Fashion-MNIST benchmarks, while requiring only a fraction of the number of logic gates. We achieve 98.92% on the MNIST dataset with two layers of 8000 gates. With only one layer of 8000 gates, we obtain 98.45%, showing that our method requires almost 50 times fewer gates compared to fixed-connection LGNs. Training stability up to ten layers has been ensured by employing a high learning rate, straight-through estimators and trimming constant-output gate types. Additionally, we present a LUT neuron description that enables stable training with backpropagation, tested up to 6-layer deep networks. The model requires four times fewer trainable parameters and still achieves a higher accuracy compared to the fixed-connection LGN training algorithm. Our connection-training algorithm also works well for the LUTNs, achieving an accuracy of 98.88% for two layers of 2000 6-input LUTs.

</details>


### [191] [On-Device Adaptive Battery Power Prediction for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09400)
*Avik Bhatnagar,Anton Paule,Tobias Schuermann,Sebastian Reiter,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向电动汽车电池功率预测的在线/离线模型自适应方法，使预训练模型能在资源受限设备上持续适应新数据分布。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦电动汽车（EV）电池功率预测的时间序列任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；虽然涉及"on-device learning"和资源受限场景，但轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）未提及，且应用场景与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列预测（电池功率预测）；应用场景：电动汽车（EV）动力管理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及"pretrained models"和"adaptable versions"。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。仅提及"on-device learning"和"model adaptation"策略（在线/离线自适应），属于模型持续学习/域自适应范畴，非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 提及"resource-constrained EV systems"和"on-device learning"，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：Mean Absolute Error (MAE)，在线自适应降低7.49%，离线自适应降低14.88%。

Experiments: 实验对比了在线（online）和离线（offline）两种模型自适应策略，在不同模型和时间范围上验证了预测性能提升。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测模型或轻量化技术；3. 应用场景（EV电池）与星上遥感场景差异巨大；4. 未提供计算资源消耗指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：论文提出的"on-device adaptation"思想——即预训练模型在部署后持续适应新数据分布——对遥感卫星在轨场景有一定启发意义。遥感卫星在轨后，由于光照、季节、传感器退化等因素，数据分布也会发生漂移，可借鉴其在线自适应策略来更新检测模型。但该论文本身不涉及目标检测和轻量化，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Adaptive power management in Electric Vehicles (EVs) requires accurate power prediction. Although deep learning models have emerged as highly effective for time-series forecasting in this domain, their performance is prone to degradation when exposed to data with distributions different from the training data. We introduce a novel approach that enables on-device learning in resource-constrained EV systems to continuously adapt pretrained battery prediction models to new, unseen data. We leverage existing pretrained models by transforming them into adaptable versions that retain critical hyperparameter knowledge from their initial training. We comprehensively investigate both online and offline model adaptation strategies. Our results demonstrate significant improvements in forecasting performance across various models and time horizons, achieving mean absolute error reductions of up to 7.49\% and 14.88\% with online and offline adaptation techniques, respectively. This study highlights the substantial benefit of on-device adaptation, resulting in enhanced battery power predictions than unadapted model deployments in real-world EV scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [Data-Efficient Deep Learning: Empirical Guidelines for Training Set Size Estimation in Inertial Sensor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09402)
*Ofir Kruzel,Itzik Klien*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出惯性传感器分类任务中学习曲线收敛速率的经验公式，用于估算达到目标精度所需的最小样本量，核心是数据效率优化而非模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于惯性传感器（inertial sensor）的人体活动识别和手机位置识别，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可能的间接联系是"数据效率"概念，但与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化部署无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：惯性传感器分类（human activity recognition, smartphone location recognition）；传感器类型：惯性传感器（accelerometer, gyroscope等）；目标类别：人体活动/位置类别；场景：地面移动设备感知，非遥感卫星场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及"deep learning models"作为分类器。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署等任何轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6个真实世界惯性测量数据集，总计102.7小时；评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy）、学习曲线收敛速率、稳定性点（stability point，定义为学习曲线稳定到渐近最大值一定平均绝对百分比偏差内所需的样本量）。

Experiments: 在6个数据集上验证了准确率随样本量呈对数增长模式；提出了稳定性点度量指标；发现模型在远少于传统启发式方法建议的样本量下即可达到实际稳定。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 仅针对惯性传感器分类任务，结论的泛化性到遥感图像领域未验证；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 未讨论星上/边缘部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：1）"数据效率优化"概念——在遥感在轨场景中，星上标注数据稀缺，可研究小样本条件下轻量检测模型的学习曲线收敛规律；2）"稳定性点"度量方法可迁移到遥感场景，用于判断在轨模型需要多少增量样本才能稳定性能。但这些属于间接启发，非直接可复现方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models dependency on large-scale inertial datasets presents a significant bottleneck in inertial sensor-based classification tasks, such as human activity recognition and smartphone location recognition. In these domains, data collection requires massive recording campaigns that are complex, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. Currently, data-driven guidelines for determining the minimum sample size required to reach a desired accuracy level do not exist. To address this gap, this study presents a systematic empirical evaluation of learning curve convergence rates in inertial classification. We introduce a unified framework that analyzes classification performance under both binary and multi-class scenarios, and derive an empirical formula to estimate performance relative to dataset size. Testing across six diverse, real-world datasets totaling 102.7 hours of inertial measurements demonstrates that accuracy follows a consistent logarithmic growth pattern, regardless of task complexity. Leveraging this finding, we propose a quantitative stability point metric, defined as the sample size required for the learning curve to stabilize within a predefined mean absolute percentage deviation of its asymptotic maximum. Our analysis reveals that models often reach practical stability with substantially fewer samples than traditional heuristics suggest. Ultimately, we offer a generalizable framework to extrapolate total data requirements from small-scale pilot studies, optimizing the tradeoff between recording effort and model reliability. These findings shift the prevailing paradigm from maximizing data volume toward optimizing data efficiency, offering concrete, data-backed guidelines for planning recording campaigns in inertial sensing applications.

</details>


### [193] [SYNRARE: Synthetic Rare Disease EHR Generation for ML Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09404)
*Nicolai Dinh Khang Truong,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SYNRARE，一个基于Synthea框架的图形界面工具，用于生成模拟罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录数据，以支持机器学习算法的基准测试。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于医学健康信息学领域，研究的是合成电子健康记录生成以辅助罕见病诊断。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：合成数据生成（Synthetic Data Generation）；应用场景：罕见病诊断、电子健康记录（EHR）数据增强；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文核心贡献是SYNRARE图形界面工具，基于Synthea框架，不涉及深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成电子健康记录（Synthetic EHR），基于Synthea框架生成；评价指标：摘要未说明具体的评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体的实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。仅提到SYNRARE使研究者能够快速基准测试其机器学习算法。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。该论文面向医学领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医学信息学中的合成数据生成，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题域、数据模态、技术方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Motivation: Rare disease (RD) diagnosis is frequently delayed due to the similarities in symptoms to common disease variants. Machine Learning Algorithms applied to Electronic Health Records show promise for accelerating the diagnosis; however, legal and privacy concerns pose significant barriers. To address these issues, Synthetic Data Generation is an alternative method for obtaining Electronic Health Records and can be applied with any Machine Learning algorithm for benchmarking and development purposes. Despite the availability of Synthetic Data Generation algorithms, support for generating a subset of patients that differ in a definable degree from the majority to simulate patients with RD is often lacking.
  Results: We present SYNRARE, a graphical user interface based on the Synthea framework that enables easier modification and generation of synthetic Electronic Health Records of RD patients, which differ only to a definable degree from patients with common diseases, thereby enabling the benchmarking and testing of algorithms under controlled technical conditions. SYNRARE enables researchers to rapidly benchmark their Machine Learning algorithms across any scenario.
  Availability and implementation: SYNRARE, including detailed instructions for installing, is available at https://gitlab.sdu.dk/screen4care/synrare.

</details>


### [194] [Action-Factored Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Scalable Quantum Device Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09422)
*Edwin De Nicolo,Rahul Marchand,Cornelius Carlsson,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QADAPT框架，利用在线学习的因子化动作空间表示来解耦多智能体，实现量子点阵列的零样本泛化调优。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于量子计算领域的量子点阵列调优问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为量子计算中的多智能体强化学习调优，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多智能体强化学习（cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning）；应用场景：量子点阵列调优（tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: QADAPT框架，采用因子化动作空间表示（factored representation of the action space），在线学习解耦智能体，基于局部观测和奖励学习共享策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：收敛步数（convergence steps）、零样本泛化能力（zero-shot generalization）。

Experiments: 实现了对未见过的量子设备尺寸的零样本泛化（zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes），收敛步数近似恒定。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或检测相关内容。摘要未说明计算开销、实际部署约束等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。因子化动作空间解耦多智能体干扰的思路属于强化学习领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化方向无关联。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning is well suited to problems with large parameter spaces and exploitable local structure, such as the tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays. However, if parameter cross-talk is strong, a non-stationary environment from the perspective of any individual agent can destabilize learning - the same effect that plagues manual tuning of such systems. We propose using a factored representation of the action space, learned online, to decouple agents and minimize their interference. Our framework, QADAPT, uses this factorization to efficiently learn shared policies based on local measurements and rewards. With this modular strategy, we achieve zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes and maintain an approximately constant number of convergence steps to reach target regimes. This work provides a scalable route toward the rapid calibration of large-scale quantum processors.

</details>


### [195] [Active rejection enables reliable generalization of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09456)
*Mingxiang Luo,Xinnan Mao,Lu Wang,Lei Bai,Feng Ding,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师路由(ATR)方法，利用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练通用机器学习原子间势(uMLIP)教师模型，通过主动拒绝机制构建大规模高保真伪标签数据集，用于轻量级CHGNet预训练。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于计算材料科学/机器学习原子间势领域，涉及多教师知识蒸馏和主动学习，但完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于材料科学/计算化学领域，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高保真原子间势的伪标签数据构建与模型预训练；应用场景：材料科学、分子动力学模拟；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多教师路由架构：多个预训练uMLIP作为教师，轻量级CHGNet作为学生模型。核心模块包括结构描述符、教师身份编码、教师间不一致性估计模块，以及基于不确定性的路由决策模块。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏(Knowledge Distillation)思想：利用多个教师模型生成伪标签来训练轻量级学生模型CHGNet。但蒸馏目标不是模型压缩/加速，而是解决高保真训练数据不足问题。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到学生模型CHGNet是"lightweight"，但未给出具体数值。与星上/边缘部署完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用少量真实r²SCAN标签（仅占候选结构的0.2%），生成289万个r²SCAN级伪标签用于预训练；评估基准：MP-r²SCAN benchmark、held-out r²SCAN structures；评估指标：能量-力预测准确性、有限温度分子动力学轨迹稳定性。

Experiments: 实验结果表明：ATR训练出的轻量级CHGNet在held-out结构和MP-r²SCAN基准上持续优于基线和非路由控制方法；在有限温度分子动力学中，ATR提升了多个材料体系的动力学鲁棒性，基线模拟发生灾难性结构坍塌时ATR仍保持稳定轨迹。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未说明轻量级CHGNet的具体参数量、推理速度等量化指标；3. 方法设计针对材料科学中能量-力预测任务，与目标检测任务差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 虽然领域不同，但以下思路可抽象借鉴：1. 多教师路由+主动拒绝机制：在遥感目标检测中，可考虑用多个预训练检测模型（如不同backbone的YOLO变体）作为教师，通过不确定性估计筛选高质量伪标签，用于轻量级学生模型的蒸馏训练；2. 结构-教师对可靠性估计：类似地，在遥感场景中可设计图像特征描述符与教师模型能力的匹配度评估，决定哪些样本适合用哪个教师生成伪标签；3. 少量真实标签驱动大规模伪标签生成：在星上标注数据稀缺的场景下，可用少量人工标注的遥感图像校准多个教师模型，生成大规模伪标签用于轻量化检测模型的在轨更新。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) bridge quantum-mechanical accuracy and large-scale molecular dynamics, but the cost of high-accuracy calculations such as r$^2$SCAN limits training to datasets that remain small relative to the open materials space. Strong average benchmark performance also does not guarantee reliable energy--force predictions for every structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR), which reformulates high-fidelity data construction as a structure-wise decision problem under uncertainty. Using a small set of real r$^2$SCAN labels, ATR calibrates multiple pretrained uMLIP teachers and combines structural descriptors, teacher identity, and inter-teacher disagreement to estimate the reliability of each structure--teacher pair. It selects high-confidence predictions for pseudo-label generation and rejects structures for which no teacher is sufficiently reliable. With real r$^2$SCAN labels for only 0.2\% of candidate structures, ATR distils 2.89 million traceable r$^2$SCAN-level pseudo-labels for pretraining. On held-out r$^2$SCAN structures and the MP-r$^2$SCAN benchmark, a lightweight CHGNet trained on the ATR-generated dataset consistently outperforms the baseline and non-routed controls. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics further shows that ATR improves dynamical robustness across multiple material systems, maintaining stable trajectories where baseline simulations undergo catastrophic structural collapse. These results establish active rejection as an effective mechanism for converting multiple pretrained uMLIPs into a scalable and reliable data-construction system for high-fidelity uMLIPs.

</details>


### [196] [All Explanations are Wrong, But Many Are Useful: Exploring the Rashomon Explanation Set with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09502)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon Explanation范式及RashomonLLM框架，通过解释-预测-反思的智能体流程将解释与预测耦合，证明解释保真度与模型性能正相关，在多个非遥感任务上同时提升准确率和可解释性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于可解释AI（XAI）的准确率-可解释性权衡问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。应用场景为客户流失分类、临床生存回归、工业点击率预测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可解释机器学习（XAI）中的解释生成与预测耦合。应用场景：客户流失分类、临床生存回归、工业点击率预测（大规模直播流日志）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: RashomonLLM框架，基于Explanation-Prediction-Reflection智能体工作流，使用自然语言生成解释，通过迭代对齐解释与预测来探索Rashomon解释集。核心组件为LLM（大语言模型）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。考虑到使用LLM作为核心组件，计算开销可能较大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：客户流失分类数据集、临床生存回归数据集、大规模直播流日志数据集。评价指标：准确率（accuracy）、解释质量（explanation quality）、解释保真度（explanation fidelity）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和指标细节。

Experiments: 在三个任务上RashomonLLM显著优于SOTA预测和XAI基线，准确率和解释质量均有提升，增益由解释保真度驱动，对分布偏移、时间分割和随机种子具有鲁棒性。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，与在轨目标检测无关；2. 使用LLM作为核心组件，计算开销大，不满足轻量化需求；3. 未讨论模型在资源受限设备上的部署可行性；4. 未涉及目标检测任务，仅涉及分类/回归任务。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的启发是：如果未来在遥感在轨目标检测中需要可解释性，可以考虑"解释与预测耦合"的思想，但当前框架基于LLM，计算开销过大，不适合星上场景。不建议在本课题中深入参考此论文。

Abstract: Explaining machine-learning models is increasingly important for decision-making and consumer trust, yet it is widely believed to come at a cost: existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods suffer from a persistent accuracy-explainability trade-off. We argue that this trade-off is not fundamental, but an artifact of treating explanation and prediction as separate objectives; when properly coupled, they become complementary, so that equipping a model to explain itself improves, rather than degrades, its accuracy. We introduce the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which builds a set of faithful, prediction-guiding explanations rather than a single one, and prove that this set is generally non-empty and that explanation fidelity bounds the performance of the models it guides. To explore this set, we propose RashomonLLM, an Explanation-Prediction-Reflection agentic workflow that generates explanations in natural language by iteratively aligning them with predictions, and we prove it converges and recovers the full set. Across customer-churn classification, clinical survival regression, and industrial click-through prediction on large-scale live-streaming logs, RashomonLLM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prediction and XAI baselines on both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by explanation fidelity and robust to distribution shifts, temporal splits, and seeds. Our framework thus advances business performance while laying the groundwork for consumer trust.

</details>


### [197] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦元宇宙虚拟经济中的欺诈检测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TSAI-MetaFraud（自建多模态基准数据集）。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标。实验设置：使用机器学习模型和图神经网络进行基线评估。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明提供了基线评估，未给出具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验数据。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文面向元宇宙欺诈分析，属于完全不同的研究领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何交集，不建议进一步阅读。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [198] [GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09537)
*Qitai Tan,Ruiwen Gu,Yilin Su,Mo Li,Xu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GatedLinear轻量级时序预测框架，通过三因子融合门控机制自适应路由互补线性基，实现高精度、可解释且小参数量的时间序列预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用时间序列预测（time series forecasting），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未提及星上部署或边缘推理。虽然论文提出了轻量化框架（lightweight framework）并关注参数量（smaller parameter footprint），但轻量化是服务于时序预测任务的通用方法，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化需求无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不推荐深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列预测（time series forecasting）；应用场景：通用时序数据（如经济、气象等），非遥感场景；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: GatedLinear框架包含三个专用线性基：全局趋势-季节基（global trend-seasonal basis）、差分增量基（difference-based incremental basis）、相位对齐循环基（phase-aligned recurrence basis）；核心模块为Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate（三因子融合门控），将路由决策分解为通道偏好（channel-specific preferences）、水平感知偏移（horizon-aware offsets）和相位索引偏置（phase-indexed biases）。

Lightweight method: 轻量网络设计（lightweight framework），通过线性基池（pool of complementary linear bases）替代计算密集型神经网络模块（computationally heavy neural modules），参数量显著小于复杂基础模型（substantially smaller parameter footprint）。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提及"substantially smaller parameter footprint"（参数量显著更小），但无量化数据。未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署方案，未讨论实时性或星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及"standard benchmarks"（标准基准数据集）。

Experiments: 在标准基准上达到state-of-the-art或高度竞争性的准确率，与近期复杂基础模型对比，提供可解释的路由模式（interpretable routing patterns）。摘要未给出消融实验或详细对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未提供具体参数量、FLOPs等轻量化量化指标；3. 仅针对时序预测任务，不涉及检测任务中的空间特征提取和定位；4. 未讨论在边缘设备或星上硬件的实际部署验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 三因子融合门控（Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate）的设计思路——将路由决策分解为多个维度的偏好因子——可借鉴到遥感目标检测的多尺度特征融合或旋转框解耦预测头中；2. 轻量线性基池替代重型模块的思想，可启发遥感检测模型中用轻量线性变换替代部分卷积或注意力操作；3. 但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.

</details>


### [199] [CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09543)
*Gabriel Mahuas,Victoria Shevchenko,Ugo Tanielian,Yassir Bendou,Richard Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对比预训练的EEG解码模型CoCoT，采用多尺度时序卷积+Transformer编码器，在脑电信号解码任务上超越现有重建预训练方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是脑电信号(EEG)解码，属于生物医学信号处理领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能没有任何关联。不涉及目标检测、轻量化、星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

Model architecture: 多尺度时序卷积输入层 + Transformer编码器块（CoCoT）。摘要未说明具体层数、通道数等细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Experiments: CoCoT在多个EEG解码基准任务上匹配或超越SOTA重建预训练模型；从头训练的CoCoT优于以往单任务解码模型甚至媲美预训练模型；进行了模型架构和预训练目标的系统消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物医学信号处理领域，与遥感目标检测和轻量化部署的研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.

</details>


### [200] [Graph-Regularized Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Variable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09546)
*Benoît Loucheur,P. -A. Absil,Michel Journée*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种图正则化的黎曼信赖域矩阵补全方法（GR-RTRMC），利用矩阵行列间的内在关系提升低秩矩阵补全的精度和鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于低秩矩阵补全这一数学优化问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：低秩矩阵补全（low-rank matrix completion）；应用场景：通用矩阵补全；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于RTRMC（Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion）框架，引入图正则化（graph regularization），在Grassmann流形上进行无约束优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。未提及具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。未提供实验对比或消融结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。图正则化思想本身可作为一种通用正则化手段，但该论文未涉及遥感场景或检测任务，且未讨论任何轻量化或部署问题，对本课题无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: We address the low-rank matrix completion problem by incorporating graph regularization into the existing Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion (RTRMC) framework. The latter uses the geometry of the low-rank constraint to remodel the problem as an unconstrained optimization problem on a single Grassmann manifold. Our approach, named Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), exploits the inherent relationships between rows and columns of the matrix. By using these relationships, we aim to improve the accuracy and robustness of matrix completion, particularly in scenarios where the underlying data exhibits strong correlations between rows or columns.

</details>


### [201] [Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09641)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多目标强化学习（Semantic Pareto-DQN）的金融异常检测方法，利用大语言模型生成语义状态表示，解决类别极度不平衡下的"欺诈崩溃"问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦金融异常检测（fraud detection），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然类别不平衡问题在遥感小目标检测中也有出现，但该文方法（多目标强化学习+LLM语义编码）与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化需求无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融异常检测（fraud detection）；应用场景：电子商务欺诈检测、信用欺诈检测；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：欺诈交易 vs 正常交易。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Semantic Pareto-DQN：多目标强化学习框架，使用大语言模型（LLM）将异构交易特征编码为自然语言叙事作为状态表示，DQN作为强化学习智能体，优化向量化奖励（Pareto前沿）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且该方法依赖LLM编码，计算开销大，与星上/边缘部署的轻量化需求背道而驰。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：E-Commerce fraud dataset、UCI Credit dataset；评价指标：minority-class recall（少数类召回率），对比scalarized baselines。不涉及遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、SSDD、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 实验表明Semantic Pareto-DQN成功打破零召回陷阱，在少数类召回率上优于标量化基线方法，能够以可控的操作摩擦换取更好的异常发现能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 依赖大语言模型进行特征编码，计算和存储开销大，不适合资源受限的星上部署场景；3. 仅在金融数据集上验证，未在遥感图像上测试；4. 未讨论模型轻量化或实时推理问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1）类别不平衡问题在遥感小目标检测中同样严重，该文的多目标优化思路（Pareto前沿权衡漏检与虚警）可迁移到遥感检测的损失函数设计；2）但该方法依赖LLM，计算量过大，不适合星上部署，需寻找更轻量的语义表示方式。总体而言，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

</details>

### [202] [Matched Generators for the Karhunen--Loève Transform: A Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08788)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从数学上研究Karhunen-Loève变换（KLT）与经典变换（傅里叶、余弦、Mellin、球谐）之间的对称性关系，提出通过双交换子特征值问题寻找与协方差矩阵近似交换的生成元，从而恢复或插值经典变换，并分析了编码代价与对称性适应的关系。

Abstract: The Karhunen--Loève transform (KLT) diagonalizes the covariance of a second-order process and is optimal for mean-square truncation. Which classical transform it reduces to is governed by the symmetry commutant of the covariance: when the kernel commutes with a group action, the KLT eigenfunctions are the irreducible representation functions of that group, recovering the Fourier, cosine, Mellin, and spherical-harmonic systems. We study the inverse question. Given a covariance $R$ and a finite-dimensional space of candidate generators, the generator nearest to commuting with $R$, the minimizer of $δ(A,R)=\|[R,A]\|_F/(\|R\|_F\|A\|_F)$, is the smallest-eigenvalue solution of a double-commutator eigenvalue problem $\mathrm{ad}_R^2(A^\ast)=λA^\ast$, a Hermitian generalized eigenvalue problem of size the number of generators, independent of dimension. The framework recovers hidden transforms as well as classical ones: a variational characterization turns the existence of a commuting generator into a spectral condition, and a tridiagonal commutant-uniqueness result yields the prolate spheroidal, cosine, and discrete orthogonal-polynomial bases as exact recoveries, with matrix-valued extensions, and produces a continuum of transforms interpolating between and beyond the classical families. When symmetry is approximate, the coding penalty of the symmetry-adapted blockwise transform equals the multi-information among the sectors, an exact threshold between the fixed and data-driven transforms. We further give a graph-automorphism characterization of permutation structure, a sequential deflation for non-Abelian symmetry, and stability bounds under estimation error. As an application, the KLT of a two-paradigm covariance is synthesized from its two known generators, without forming the mixed covariance, reaching the full-data transform's compaction from few observations.

</details>


### [203] [A 2.4 GHz LC-VCO Fractional-N Phase Locked Loop Open-Source Design in 130-nm BiCMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08852)
*Manimohan Thiriloganathan,Shenal Ranasinghe,Avishka Herath,Rajinthan Rameshkumar,Hansa Marasinghe,Anjana Viduranga,Gayangana Leelarathne,Kithmin Wickremasinghe*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于开源CMOS工艺的2.4 GHz分数-N锁相环（PLL）设计，重点在于片上螺旋电感建模与LC-VCO实现，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) integrated circuit design using the open-source complementary Metal-Oxide semiconductor (CMOS) ecosystem, such as for phase-locked loops (PLLs), is limited by the absence of reliable passive device models, particularly on-chip spiral inductors. Consequently, prior work relies on ring-oscillator-based voltage-controlled oscillators (VCOs) with degraded phase noise performance. This work presents a 2.4 GHz type-II fractional-N PLL implemented in the IHP SG13G2 130 nm BiCMOS open-source technology. The proposed design employs a cross-coupled differential LC-VCO integrated with a custom-designed spiral inductor, developed using an open-source electromagnetic modelling workflow in OpenEMS. The optimized inductor achieves 4 nH inductance with a quality factor of 16.8 at 2.45 GHz. The LC-VCO sensitivity is approximately 120 MHz/V, while the PLL phase noise is -100.8 dBc/Hz at 1 MHz offset. The complete PLL is realized using a fully open-source electronic design automation (EDA) flow, occupying a total area of 930 um x 666 um (~0.619 mm2) and consuming 12.73 mW, demonstrating the feasibility of RF integrated circuit design in an open-source CMOS IC design ecosystem.

</details>


### [204] [Fast Pinching-Antenna Activation for AirComp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08873)
*Zhenqiao Cheng,Boqun Zhao,Chongjun Ouyang,Xingqi Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文研究基于pinching天线系统的空中计算（AirComp）问题，聚焦于离散天线激活位置选择以最小化均方误差，与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是无线通信领域的pinching天线系统（PASS）和空中计算（AirComp），属于通信信号处理方向。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。
