Abstract: A pinching-antenna system (PASS) is considered for over-the-air computation (AirComp). Multiple dielectric waveguides are deployed at the base station, and one pinching antenna (PA) is activated on each waveguide. For practical implementation, each PA is restricted to a finite set of preconfigured locations. The resulting discrete activation problem is formulated to minimize the AirComp mean-squared error (MSE). After the optimal aggregation vector is derived, the minimum MSE is expressed through an inverse Gram matrix. A rank-one recursion is derived to evaluate the exact MSE reduction produced by each candidate. Greedy search and beam search are then developed for fast tree search. To further reduce complexity, coherent aggregation search is proposed from a first-order MSE approximation. It admits a separable closed-form selection rule and is asymptotically optimal in the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime. Numerical results show that the proposed methods substantially improve the AirComp accuracy of PASS over conventional antenna arrays.

</details>


### [205] [Every Sample Counts: Supervised Fine-Tuning of Language Models with Pointwise Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08968)
*Ignacio Hounie,Ignacio Boero,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向语言模型微调的逐样本约束对齐框架，通过可学习的样本依赖松弛机制和增广拉格朗日方法，在保持模型性能的同时减少尾部约束违规。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题设定、技术方法、应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models often requires enforcing constraints on individual inputs without compromising downstream performance. Existing constrained alignment methods impose constraints on average, which can induce undesirable disparities across inputs or users. We propose a novel alignment framework that addresses this gap by enforcing per-sample constraints while still minimizing an average loss. To mitigate the impact of overly restrictive constraints and outliers, we introduce a learned, sample-dependent relaxation that minimally adjusts the constraints, trading off a user-defined relaxation cost with the training objective. To address practical duality and optimization challenges, we develop an augmented Lagrangian approach tailored to this formulation. We demonstrate the flexibility of the framework by instantiating it under distinct small language-model fine-tuning tasks and constraints: safety in instruction following, preferences in function calling and length in re-ranking. Across these settings, our approach reduces tail constraint violations while largely preserving the model's performance.

</details>


### [206] [Inter-frame Channel Prediction for Zak-OTFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09184)
*Muhammad Ubadah,Saif Khan Mohammed*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种Zak-OTFS调制下的帧间信道预测方法，利用确定性变化和子空间不变性实现未来帧的信道预测。

Abstract: Zak-Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (OTFS) modulation is known to be robust to Doppler spread in high mobility scenarios when compared to Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM). This is due to the fact that the channel response to a Zak-OTFS carrier within a frame can be accurately estimated from the channel response to another carrier within the same frame. However, an important open problem and question is whether inter-frame channel prediction is possible with Zak-OTFS, i.e., is it possible to accurately predict the channel response to a Zak-OTFS carrier in a frame based on knowledge of the channel response to some Zak-OTFS carrier in \emph{another} frame (i.e., not the same frame).
  In this paper we show that indeed inter-frame channel prediction is possible. We show that the effective DD domain channel filter coefficients vary in a deterministic manner as we move from current to future frames in time and frequency. We also show that the subspace spanned by channel filter coefficients of consecutive frames in time/frequency is invariant to discrete shifts in time and frequency. We exploit the deterministic variation and subspace invariance to propose a novel deterministic ESPIRIT-type method which uses the effective DD domain channel filter taps/coefficients estimated in training frames (i.e., current/past frames in time and frequency having both pilot and data carriers) to predict the effective DD domain channel filter for frames which are several tens of frames in future and several tens of frames away in frequency.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出一种面向低轨卫星互联网的免授权随机接入方案，通过多域协同稀疏性和残差反馈机制提升活跃用户检测与信道估计性能。

Research relevance: 1分。论文主题是低轨卫星通信中的免授权随机接入与信号检测（AUD/CE/DD），属于卫星通信物理层领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。虽然涉及"卫星"和"检测"（active user detection），但此检测非彼检测（目标检测），且不涉及任何计算机视觉、深度学习模型压缩或边缘部署内容。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于卫星通信物理层信号处理领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化属于不同研究方向。

Abstract: Grant-Free (GF) random access is promising for low Earth orbit satellite Internet due to its reduced access latency. However, existing schemes suffer from poor performance in massive connectivity scenarios. To address this challenge, we firstly propose an iterative residual feedback multi-measurement vector approximate message passing algorithm. This algorithm leverages multi-domain synergistic sparsity in the spatial-frequency and angular-delay domains to alternately perform active user terminal detection (AUD) and channel estimation (CE). Additionally, a residual feedback mechanism is incorporated to suppress error accumulation, thereby enhancing AUD performance. Furthermore, conventional data detection (DD) methods significantly degrade when active user terminals are spatially close or outnumber the satellite's receive antennas, making the demodulation problem rank-deficient or underdetermined. To mitigate this, we design a data modulation scheme via joint spatial-frequency multi-domain spreading, which utilizes observations from both spatial and frequency domains to facilitate multi-domain DD. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms existing GF methods in terms of AUD accuracy, CE precision, and bit error rate, especially under conditions of low effective pilot length and practical signal-to-noise ratios.

</details>


### [208] [Tensor-based Random Access for Ambient IoT Contention Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09331)
*Alberto Rech,Alexis Decurninge,Ala Baccar,Sofiane Kharbech*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于张量分解的无授权随机接入方案，用于环境物联网（A-IoT）场景下的初始接入消息传输，通过张量分解和串行干扰消除实现联合活动检测、信道估计与解码。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于环境物联网（A-IoT）的通信接入层设计，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"卫星"可能出现在物联网场景中，但本文讨论的是地面A-IoT设备的随机接入协议，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 基于张量分解（tensor decomposition）和串行干扰消除（successive interference cancellation, SIC）的接收机架构。发射端将短载荷组织为rank-1张量并在多个接入时机发送副本。

Abstract: Ambient Internet of Things (A-IoT) deployments impose stringent hardware constraints, including low-order modulations and simple transceiver architectures. We propose a tensor-based grant-free random access scheme for the initial access message (Msg1) tailored to these limitations. Each device transmits replicas of its short payload structured as a rank-1 tensor over multiple access occasions. The A-IoT receiver performs joint activity detection, channel estimation, and decoding via tensor decomposition and successive interference cancellation. Results show significant throughput gains over conventional schemes while preserving low-complexity transmitter designs.

</details>


### [209] [Site Geometry and Calibration Uncertainties in Digital Twin-enabled Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09334)
*Lorenzo Del Moro,Francesco Linsalata,Umberto Spagnolini,Maurizio Magarini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究数字孪生辅助信道估计中，射线追踪提取的多径特征用于构建低秩信道协方差矩阵，并分析用户定位误差和电磁材料校准误差对估计鲁棒性的影响。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信领域的数字孪生辅助信道估计问题，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"ray tracing"和"digital twin"，但应用场景是环境感知通信，而非遥感卫星在轨智能处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：数字孪生辅助信道估计；场景：无线通信（urban, suburban, rural）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: 基于射线追踪提取多径特征，构建信道协方差矩阵的低秩特征结构，并建立一阶扰动模型分离几何扰动（角度、时延）和电磁扰动（路径增益）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：归一化均方误差（NMSE）。数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集，使用urban, suburban, rural场景的仿真数据。

Experiments: 数值实验表明：定位误差是性能下降的主要因素，电磁校准误差影响有限；在urban低SNR场景下，DT辅助估计器相比基线方法有高达10dB的NMSE提升；在中等定位误差（<1m）的高SNR下，性能与基线方法相当。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文聚焦无线信道估计的鲁棒性分析，不涉及任何视觉感知、目标检测或模型压缩问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文研究方向（无线通信信道估计）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。如果强行关联，数字孪生中的模型失配分析思路或许对遥感仿真数据与真实数据之间的域适应问题有间接启发，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Fast ray tracing (RT) has stimulated the Digital Twin (DT) as an emerging technology for environment-aware communications. Since wireless propagation is governed by the interaction between site geometry and electromagnetic (EM) properties of the environment, DT-based approaches can provide site-specific prior information for channel estimation. In this work, we investigate the robustness of DT to aid the channel estimation, where multipath features extracted via RT are used to construct the low-rank (LR) eigenstructure of the channel covariance matrix. This LR structure is used in channel estimation. However, the digital representation of propagation model is inaccurate and thus it affects the LR. We explicitly analyze these model mismatches that arise from user positioning errors, which translate into geometric inconsistencies in the site representation, and EM material calibration errors. We derive a first-order perturbative model that separates geometric perturbations, affecting angles and delays, from EM perturbations, affecting path gains. Based on this perturbed model, we provide a normalized mean-square error (NMSE) analysis that reveals a fundamental difference between geometric and EM perturbations. In particular, we show that LR estimation is inherently robust to EM calibration perturbations, while positioning errors, dominate performance degradation by altering the channel eigenstructure. Numerical results confirm that, in urban, suburban and rural scenarios, positioning errors are the primary limiting factor, whereas EM calibration errors have a comparatively limited impact. Despite these mismatches, DT-empowered estimators provide up to 10dB NMSE improvement, over baseline methods, in the urban low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) settings, while achieving performance comparable to baseline estimators at high SNR for moderate (< 1 m) positioning errors.

</details>


### [210] [Drone-Based Antenna Measurement System with Optimized Positioning and ASPIRE-Based NF-FF Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09361)
*Simranjit Singh,Abha Nilesh Jadav,Aarish Dharmesh Patel,Jaswant,Jigar M. Pandya*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究基于无人机的天线测量系统，通过优化无人机组件选型和ASPIRE算法提升近远场变换精度，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是UAV-based antenna measurement（无人机天线测量），属于电磁场与天线测试领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了UAV平台，但目的是天线近远场变换，而非遥感成像或目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：UAV天线近场测量与远场重建；场景：天线测试场；传感器类型：天线探头（非遥感光学/SAR/高光谱）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、Earth observation相关内容。

Model architecture: ASPIRE (Adaptive Sparse Inverse Radiation Estimation) 算法，基于稀疏信号恢复（sparse signal recovery）实现近远场变换。摘要未说明深度学习模型或神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。ASPIRE算法使用稀疏表示（仅24%的RWG网格作为active support），但这属于信号处理层面的稀疏性，而非深度学习模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验频率：6.7125 GHz；指标：残差1.94%、波束宽度误差0.4度、active support占比24%（17,298-element RWG mesh）。无遥感目标检测常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）和指标（mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 与传统天线测试设施对比，ASPIRE算法在6.7125 GHz下残差1.94%，波束宽度误差0.4度，仅使用24%的RWG网格。摘要未说明消融实验或与其他NF-FF变换方法的对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。属于天线测量领域的专用信号处理算法。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。稀疏信号恢复的思想（ASPIRE）在理论上与遥感图像稀疏表示有共通之处，但本文未涉及遥感图像处理或目标检测，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV)-based antenna measurement systems provide a flexible and cost-effective alternative to conventional antenna test ranges for characterizing large and installed antennas. However, their accuracy depends on precise UAV positioning and efficient flight-time utilization, both of which are strongly influenced by the selection of drone assemblies, including the airframe, flight controller, propulsion system, positioning modules, and onboard instrumentation. This paper presents a comprehensive study of UAV-based antenna measurements with emphasis on improving positioning accuracy and optimizing flight endurance through systematic drone assembly selection. The acquired near-field measurement data are susceptible to positioning errors, amplitude and phase inconsistencies, and irregular sampling, which degrade the reconstructed far-field pattern. To address these challenges, the recorded near-field data are processed using the Adaptive Sparse Inverse Radiation Estimation (ASPIRE) algorithm. ASPIRE compensates for positioning inaccuracies and reconstructs the far-field pattern from irregularly sampled near-field data using sparse signal recovery, enabling accurate Near-Field to Far-Field (NF-FF) transformation. At 6.7125 GHz, ASPIRE achieves a residual of 1.94% and a beamwidth error of 0.4 degrees relative to a conventional facility measurement while using only 24% of the 17,298-element RWG mesh as active support. The results demonstrate that the combination of optimized drone assembly selection and ASPIRE-based NF-FF transformation significantly improves the accuracy of UAV-based antenna measurements and produces far-field patterns that closely agree with conventional antenna test range measurements.

</details>

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: This study investigates orbital angular momentum (OAM)-based holographic multiple-input multiple-output (HMIMO) links enabled by stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM) in the radiative near-field. By using multilayer programmable metasurfaces at both link ends, SIMs enable analog electromagnetic domain wave processing for low-complexity and energy-efficient flexible wavefront synthesis. We analyzed OAM mode generation and reception with SIM-based transceivers and quantified their ability to synthesize near-orthogonal modes in practical discrete HMIMO architectures. We further developed a correlation-driven optimization algorithm that maximizes reconstruction accuracy of OAM beams. Numerical evaluations revealed a fundamental decoupling between the required antenna aperture, which limits the supported mode orders, and the SIM layer depth, which governs crosstalk suppression. The results confirm that properly dimensioned SIM architectures provide robust near-field spatial multiplexing, nearly balanced per-mode capacities, and graceful degradation across link distances without requiring continuous phase re-optimization, thereby supporting scalable and low-overhead HMIMO communications.

</details>


### [213] [Fused Constrained Policy Reuse Optimization for Wireless Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09498)
*An Liu,Zheyuan Zhou,Kexuan Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Fused-CPRO方法，通过融合可学习策略、源策略和领域知识策略，加速无线资源分配中的深度强化学习收敛，减少在线探索成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦无线通信领域的资源分配（MU-MIMO功率控制、MIMO-ISAC波束赋形），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"轻量化"在摘要中未出现，但即使从方法层面看，知识融合策略优化与模型压缩/加速也无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无线资源分配（功率控制、波束赋形）；应用场景：无线通信系统；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: Fused-CPRO框架：将分配策略构建为可学习目标策略、相关场景源策略、领域知识专家策略的混合体，在约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）下联合优化目标策略和复用概率。使用约束随机逐次凸逼近（CSSCA）处理非凸目标和约束，使用混合离线-在线数据训练的critic提高样本效率。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。论文的核心是知识融合策略优化以加速DRL收敛，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是无线通信中的信令开销和能耗，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真场景：延迟约束多用户MIMO（MU-MIMO）功率控制、CRB约束MIMO-ISAC波束赋形。评价指标：经验性能（empirical performance）、收敛速度（convergence speed）。摘要未说明具体数据集。

Experiments: 与代表性基线方法对比，Fused-CPRO在经验性能和收敛速度上均有提升。证明了几乎必然收敛到KKT点。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 方法针对无线通信场景设计，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务；3. 摘要未讨论模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是"知识融合"的思想——在遥感目标检测模型轻量化中，可考虑融合预训练源模型的知识（如蒸馏）和领域先验知识（如遥感目标的几何先验）来加速轻量化模型的训练收敛。但该论文的方法框架（CMDP+CSSCA）与目标检测模型压缩差异较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has been widely adopted for wireless resource allocation due to its model-free adaptability. However, online exploration is costly, as randomly initialized policies may violate long-term constraints before sufficient data are collected. Future wireless systems must cope with increasingly dynamic traffic, fluctuating channel conditions, and stringent energy efficiency requirements, demanding algorithms that can learn quickly with minimal environment interactions to reduce both energy consumption and signaling overhead. We develop Fused-CPRO, a knowledge-fused constrained policy reuse optimization method addressing these challenges. Fused-CPRO constructs the allocation policy as a mixture of a learnable target policy, source policies from related scenarios, and domain-knowledge (DK) policies from expert rules, jointly optimizing the target policy and reuse probabilities under a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). This fusion of heterogeneous priors accelerates convergence and enhances robustness. Constrained stochastic successive convex approximation (CSSCA) handles non-convex objectives and constraints, while a critic trained from mixed offline-online data improves sample efficiency by reusing pre-collected experience. We prove almost-sure convergence to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) point. Simulations on delay-constrained multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) power control and Cramer-Rao bound (CRB)-constrained multiple-input multiple-output integrated sensing and communication (MIMO-ISAC) beamforming demonstrate that Fused-CPRO improves empirical performance and converges substantially faster than representative baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Symbol-Level Precoding for Continuous-Aperture ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09610)
*Hongli Liu,Qiang Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究连续孔径阵列（CAPA）在下行ISAC系统中的符号级预编码与极化合并优化问题，提出基于电磁响应子空间的有限维重构方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通信感知一体化（ISAC）中的连续孔径阵列优化，属于无线通信与信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：ISAC系统中的符号级预编码与极化合并优化；场景：下行通信与目标感知；传感器类型：连续孔径阵列（CAPA）；目标类别：通信用户与感知目标（非遥感目标检测中的舰船/飞机/车辆等）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。核心是优化连续电流分布，利用电磁响应子空间将无限维问题转化为有限维系数优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作属于通信信号处理优化，未考虑星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：目标照射功率（sensing utility）和通信可靠性（communication reliability）。

Experiments: 仿真实验表明所提框架相比传统Fourier-basis CAPA和空间离散阵列基线，在感知效用和通信可靠性上均有提升。

Limitations: 1. 纯仿真验证，无实际硬件实验；2. 未考虑实时计算复杂度与硬件实现约束；3. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的交叉点是：如果未来在轨遥感卫星需要同时执行通信与感知任务（如星间链路+遥感成像），ISAC优化方法可能提供参考，但与本课题"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向不直接相关。

Abstract: Continuous-aperture arrays (CAPAs) offer rich electromagnetic degrees of freedom for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC), but optimizing continuous current distributions leads to challenging infinite-dimensional problems. This paper investigates a CAPA-enabled downlink ISAC system with symbol-level precoding and receive polarization combining. The transmit current is optimized to maximize weighted target-illumination power while enforcing constructive-interference constraints for communication users. To address the resulting infinite-dimensional nonconvex problem, we establish that the optimal current distribution lies in a finite-dimensional subspace spanned by the communication and sensing electromagnetic responses. This result yields an exact, structure-preserving reformulation in terms of finite-dimensional coefficients. A penalty projected-gradient algorithm is then developed to jointly optimize the current coefficients and polarization combiners. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves higher sensing utility and improved communication reliability than conventional Fourier-basis CAPA and spatially discrete array baselines.

</details>

### [215] [Beyond Metadata: CAPRA for Hidden Subgroup Analysis under Missing Metadata in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09102)
*Yawen Li,Yan Li,Zhe Xue,Yingxia Shao,Meiyu Liang,Guanhua Ye*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出CAPRA框架，在医疗影像缺失元数据场景下，通过校准的代理轴实现隐藏子群分析，用于部署时故障分析和鲁棒学习。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦医疗影像（fundus, dermoscopy, chest radiography）的公平性/鲁棒性分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对医疗影像中的子群分析有交叉兴趣，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：隐藏子群分析（hidden subgroup analysis）、部署时故障分析（deployment-time failure analysis）、鲁棒学习（robust learning）。应用场景：医疗影像（眼底、皮肤镜、胸部X光）。传感器类型：医疗成像设备。目标类别：未涉及目标检测，关注的是子群性能差异。

Model architecture: CAPRA框架包含三个组件：1）图像语义轴预测器（image-derived semantic axes）；2）基于患者级交叉拟合的校准后验（calibrated axis posteriors via patient-level cross-fitting）；3）校准子群接口（calibrated subgroup interface）。摘要未说明具体骨干网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。虽然提及"deployment-time analysis"，但这里的部署指医疗场景下的模型部署，非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：眼底（fundus）、皮肤镜（dermoscopy）、胸部X光（chest radiography）三个医疗影像领域的数据集。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标，提及"disparity patterns"、"alignment with explicit failure axes"等定性/定量分析方式。

Experiments: 主要发现：1）CAPRA能发现元数据切片（metadata-only slicing）遗漏的差异模式；2）在数据集偏移下仍保持信息量；3）产生的子群划分比纯图像或潜在切片基线更接近显式故障轴；4）下游鲁棒学习可复用该接口，但增益具有领域依赖性。

Limitations: 1）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2）下游鲁棒学习的增益具有领域依赖性，泛化性有限；3）需要少量带元数据标签的样本进行校准，在遥感场景中可能难以获取；4）摘要未说明计算开销和实时性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1）"校准的代理轴"思想——在遥感在轨场景中，如果某些标注信息（如目标尺寸、天气条件）在推理时缺失，可考虑类似的代理预测+小样本校准策略来评估模型在不同子群上的表现；2）部署时故障分析（deployment-time failure analysis）的思路可用于在轨模型运行时的性能监控。但整体而言，该论文的方法论高度针对医疗公平性场景，与遥感目标检测轻量化的核心问题（精度-速度-参数量权衡）不直接相关。

Abstract: Medical imaging models are often deployed without the demographic, acquisition, and quality metadata needed for subgroup auditing. Once those metadata disappear, clinically critical failure modes can be masked by strong aggregate performance, and many robust-learning methods lose the group structure they rely on. We present CAPRA, a calibrated proxy-axis framework for hidden subgroup analysis under missing metadata. CAPRA predicts image-derived semantic axes, calibrates axis posteriors on a small metadata-labeled split via patient-level cross-fitting, and organizes those posteriors into a calibrated subgroup interface that supports both deployment-time failure analysis and downstream robust learning without requiring subgroup labels at deployment. Across fundus, dermoscopy, and chest radiography, CAPRA reveals disparity patterns missed by metadata-only slicing, remains informative under dataset shift, and produces subgroup partitions that align more closely with explicit failure axes than image-only or latent-slice baselines. The same interface can also be reused by downstream robust learners, although those gains are domain-dependent. Overall, CAPRA turns hidden subgroup analysis under missing metadata into a calibrated, interpretable, and reusable subgroup interface for deployment-time analysis and robust transfer.

</details>


### [216] [Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Solar PV Panel Fault Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09205)
*Seyyedhamid Azimidokht,Mehdi Monemi,Abdelhak Kharbouch,Farid Hamzehaghdam,Mehdi Rasti,Jamshid Aghaei,Emil Kurvinen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出JEFFNet，融合JEPA自监督视觉Transformer与EfficientNetV2-S监督卷积特征，用于热红外光伏故障分类，参数量减少47.2%。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及热红外遥感成像（thermal IR imaging），但应用场景是地面光伏板故障分类，非遥感卫星在轨场景；不涉及目标检测（object detection），而是图像分类（classification）；不涉及轻量化部署（虽提及参数量减少，但108.6M参数仍较大，且未涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏/边缘部署等）。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2分。与研究方向弱相关，仅热红外模态和自监督+卷积融合思路有微弱借鉴价值，不建议作为重点阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（fault classification），非目标检测。应用场景：地面光伏电站热红外故障检测。传感器类型：热红外（thermal IR）。目标类别：光伏板故障类型（10类/12类/二分类健康/故障）。

Model architecture: JEFFNet多分支架构：分支1为JEPA预训练的Vision Transformer（自监督语义表示），分支2为EfficientNetV2-S（监督卷积特征），两分支特征融合后进行分类。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及轻量化方法。仅通过模型设计（多分支融合）实现了参数量减少（108.6M vs 205.91M，减少47.2%），但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术。108.6M参数对于星上部署仍过大。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。参数量108.6M，对于星上/边缘部署而言偏大，不具备在轨可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PVF-10（10类）、InfraredSolarModules (ISM)（12类）。评价指标：F1-score、Accuracy。实验设置：多分类（10类/12类）及衍生二分类（健康/故障）。

Experiments: PVF-10 10类：F1=93.21%, Acc=94.33%；PVF-10 2类：F1=97.53%, Acc=96.41%。ISM 12类：F1=72.60%, Acc=83.88%；ISM 2类：F1=94.69%, Acc=94.78%。参数量108.6M，相比GEPFNet的205.91M减少47.2%。

Limitations: 1. 非目标检测任务，不涉及边界框回归或旋转框；2. 非遥感卫星场景，不涉及卫星在轨部署；3. 参数量108.6M仍较大，未给出FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标；4. 未涉及任何模型压缩/加速技术；5. 仅针对光伏故障分类，泛化性有限。

Ideas for my research: 1. JEPA自监督预训练+轻量卷积网络融合的思路可借鉴，但需替换为遥感目标检测任务；2. 热红外遥感在卫星上也有应用（如火灾监测），但本文是地面近距拍摄，非卫星视角；3. 参数量减少47.2%的思路可参考，但108.6M对星上仍过大，需进一步压缩至<10M；4. 自监督+监督特征融合可尝试用于遥感小目标检测的特征增强。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of solar photovoltaic (PV) systems has increased the need for reliable and scalable fault classification, as manual inspection is impractical at scale. Thermal infrared (IR) imaging provides a non-contact solution for identifying PV faults; however, accurate classification remains challenging due to class imbalance, limited texture information, and subtle thermal differences. In this work, we investigate the applicability of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) for thermal IR PV fault classification across various scenarios and propose JEFFNet (JEPA-EFFicientNet), a multibranch architecture that combines JEPA-based self-supervised representation learning with EfficientNetV2-S-based supervised convolutional feature extraction. JEFFNet fuses semantic representations from a JEPA-pretrained Vision Transformer with convolutional features from EfficientNetV2-S, enabling complementary feature learning. JEFFNet is evaluated on two public thermal IR datasets, PVF-10 and InfraredSolarModules (ISM), for both multiclass and derived binary (healthy/faulty) classification. On PVF-10, JEFFNet achieves an F1-score of $93.21$ and an accuracy of $94.33$ in the 10-class task, and an F1-score of $97.53$ and an accuracy of $96.41$ in the derived 2-class task. On ISM, JEFFNet achieves an F1-score of $72.60$ and an accuracy of $83.88$ in the 12-class task, and an F1-score of $94.69$ and an accuracy of $94.78$ in the derived 2-class task. JEFFNet also uses only 108.6M parameters versus 205.91M for GEPFNet, a 47.2\% reduction. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised semantic and supervised convolutional features provides an effective, parameter-efficient solution for thermal IR PV fault classification. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Azimi2kht/JEFFNet

</details>
